HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000190/B002","root_000955/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_04ea93050233117b9b46","text":"2. **A place-reading activated without cancelling the fruit-reading.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ت ي ن/B002` accepts *al-tīn* as a named mountain or place, while fixed `ز ي ت/B002` keeps the olive tree or fruit visible. **Structural cue:** the next oath names `ط و ر/B005`, a mountain (95:2), and the following oath names `ب ل د/B001`, a bounded tract or place (95:3). **Assigned role:** the place branch of *tīn* becomes the possible first station, while olive growth marks that station as cultivated terrain. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies consecutive spatial nouns but does not identify them as one itinerary; I infer that the two explicit places exert enough semantic pressure to activate the accepted place variant of *tīn*. **Reading change:** alongside the food reading, 95:1 can inaugurate a geographic progression from a fig/olive landscape to a mount and then a secure city.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_04f30cadb9f177952b18","text":"95:6's أجر غير ممنون sharpened the material reading of fig and olive: provision is not merely food but a whole economy of support, wage, and uncut continuation. 95:8's الحكم also made the geographic reading firmer: the oath-sites are not scenic examples but witnesses in a case. I would now keep both fruit/provision and place-map readings side by side, because later recompense and final judgment give each a distinct mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000190/B001","root_000656/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4f5054918cee79aaf061","text":"1. **Complementary modes of provision.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ت ي ن/B001` supplies an edible fruit usable fresh or dried; fixed `ز ي ت/B001` supplies extracted olive oil, and `ز ي ت/B004` makes that oil condiment, provision, or gift. **Structural cue:** the two nouns are tightly coordinated as one oath unit, while the window later moves from created human capacity (95:4) to intentional work and recompense (95:6). **Assigned role:** the fig represents food available as fruit, whereas the olive represents food whose useful liquid is extracted and supplied. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the two material pathways but does not state why they are paired; I infer that whole fruit and processed extract form a complementary provisioning system. **Reading change:** the pair reads as an economy of nourishment in two modes, not as a flat botanical list.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000190/B002","root_000656/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_732f1424303aa2f9e83d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:1:1\",\"root_000190\",[\"B002\"]],[\"95:1:2\",\"root_000656\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İncirin yer adı anlamı ve zeytinin yetiştiği arazi, sonraki dağ ve güvenli şehirle birlikte yemin dizisini coğrafi bir güzergâha dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000190/B001","root_000656/B001","root_000656/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8254a9d89c4322766038","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:1:1\",\"root_000190\",[\"B001\"]],[\"95:1:2\",\"root_000656\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İncir ve zeytin, biri doğrudan yenilen meyve, diğeri yağı çıkarılarak kullanılan ürün olarak insanın ayakta kalışını destekleyen iki tamamlayıcı geçim biçimi oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000190/B001","root_000656/B001","root_000656/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_87c2c58eaaffb3aad023","text":"1. **Edible fruit and oil as material supports of upright formation.** Lexical evidence: ت ي ن B001, edible fig; ز ي ت B001, olive oil/extract; ز ي ت B004, oil as condiment, provision, or gift; ق و م B009 in 95:4, support/mainstay/subsistence. Structural cue: the oath by two food-bearing terms precedes \"we created the human in best taqwim.\" Assigned role: fig and olive become provision images that materially underwrite the body's standing and subsistence. Abductive move: I infer that an oath-object can activate the bodily support branch of ق و م, so nourishment and proportion belong to one mechanism. Reading change: the line reads not as decorative nature imagery but as testimony from sustaining matter before human valuation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000656/B001","root_000656/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_89f9ede0aa381cf97bcf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:1:2\",\"root_000656\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Zeytindeki sıkma ve yağ çıkarma emeği, ilerideki iş ve ödül ilişkisini, emekle açığa çıkan değer modeli üzerinden önceden haber verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001","root_000190/B002","root_000656/B002","root_000955/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8a37d900a377cae0834e","text":"2. **A geographic chain of bounded holy places.** Lexical evidence: ت ي ن B002, fig as named mountain/place; ز ي ت B002, olive tree/fruit as place-linked vegetation; ط و ر B005 in 95:2, the Mount; ب ل د B001 and ء م ن B001 in 95:3, bounded land and security. Structural cue: 95:1-3 are three oath units, moving from two named/fruit-place signs, to a mountain, to \"this secure town.\" Assigned role: التين can remain fruit, but B002 allows it also to stand as a place-marker; الزيتون then joins the chain as vegetation anchoring a terrain. Abductive move: I supply the assumption that parallel oath syntax can make material names behave as coordinates. Reading change: the ayah opens a map of witnessed sites, not only a pair of foods.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B010","root_000656/B001","root_000656/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ad59f9c688aa8849a8f2","text":"3. **Extraction/anointing as a hidden preparation motif.** Lexical evidence: ز ي ت B001, extracted oil; ز ي ت B003, oiling/anointing; خ ل ق B010 in 95:4, perfuming/anointing with paste; ح س ن B002, skillful beneficent action. Structural cue: the oil term precedes creation \"in best taqwim,\" and the packet later distinguishes those whose عمل is صالح. Assigned role: olive oil's extract and anointing branch turns the material sign into preparation of a body or surface for honored use. Abductive move: I infer that repeated bodily/material treatment images can activate a rite-like preparation mechanism.
